## [UC04-Fitting-Request]The fitting request can have some states. It would appear on the Fitting Request List that is is accessible on the Fitting Manager and also on the Industry Tab for the Corporation Dashboard.
[DRAFT]- The background is orange and has pending the save on the Repository. During this stage the number of copies can be changed and it only will reflect on the cost calculations for the complete fitting considering buys for all the elements at the current market prices. [OPEN]- The background is blue tinted and the number of copies cannot be changed. There should be a new menu icon to activate the main Display Focus to move to a new page with the Fitting Request on focus.

- URL: https://trello.com/c/nwx8k9k8/93-uc04-fitting-requestthe-fitting-request-can-have-some-states-it-would-appear-on-the-fitting-request-list-that-is-is-accessible-o

## [UC][CORPORATION FITTING MANUFACTURE] Once the key data items are collected the job indicator goes to green and we can launch the request. The request should have mode data included like the pilot identifier, the corporation and any other information that will help to decide the manufacturing path. This last operation is a recursive analysis of the modules and items related to the Fitting. For each of the items we can decide the actions we like to take and the order. So for existing available items we should decide if we prefer to move them depending on distance or we should better buy them.

## [BACKEND] Fitting retrieval is a quite lengthy operation because it has to render the complete EveItem and the related data. That data is not guaranteed to be cached and available so the time to get all that data can take even 2 minutes. ## [UC04-Fitting-Request]The fitting request can have some states. It would appear on the Fitting Request List that is is accessible on the Fitting Manager and also on the Industry Tab for the Corporation Dashboard.

[OPEN]- The background is blue tinted and the number of copies cannot be changed. ## [FEATURE]Install and start the same framework versions used by master branch.

- URL: https://trello.com/c/3Vu6xmtD/972-featureinstall-and-start-the-same-framework-versions-used-by-master-branch
- Card ID: `65db1751a12d317f239c754c`
- Labels: WIP (purple), STORY (orange)

Command execute to generate a similar Angular/Node/npm framework:

- nvm install 10.16.3
- nvm alias neocom.infinity 10.16.3
- nvm use neocom.infinity
- npm install --global compodoc@0.0.41 rimraf@3.0.0 cucumber@5.1.0
- npm install

The command to start the application on the development DEV environment is:

- npm run start:server

This opens the NeoCom at address localhost:8090

## [FEATURE] Create a new MiningOperations Manager to collect mining operation data.

- URL: https://trello.com/c/6BZuwwPT/997-feature-create-a-new-miningoperations-manager-to-collect-mining-operation-data
- Card ID: `65e061039e234d699a8872f5`

The new Mining Operation Manager should do the next actions:

- When the list of mining delta is not empty it should get the previous list of complete Ore assets as the start point for ore accounting.
- It should start a new mining operation with that initial list timestamp.
- If will start collecting deltas on that new mining operation and creating a draft mining report.
- When the delta is again an empty set of resources then it should close the mining  operation with this last delta as the close timestamp.
- When the mining operation is closed we get again a complete list of the ore assets and generate a complete delta of accumulated values by ore. With this action we solve the merging of stacks and any other difference skipped during the delta processing. New final report is created agains this accumulated delta set.
- Accumulated set are created by aggregating all ore of the same type into a list by ore type from the initial set of ore assets got at the mining operation start. Then the same with the current last complete list of ore assets obtained at mining operation end. The delta between this to aggregations is the new result of the mining operation.
